The example meeting a newcomer tries: synthetic voices generated with ElevenLabs, bundled as sound files (ui/example/LICENSE):

The example meeting (ui/example/fr, ui/example/en) is an invented meeting of two voices, made by
scripts/example.py. No real meeting and no real person are in it.

Synthetic voices generated with ElevenLabs (eleven-multilingual-v2) through OpenRouter, on
2026-11-27, speed 0.95. French: the voices « lily » (« Vous ») and « eric » (Hugo). English:
« will » (« Vous ») and « matilda » (Jess).

Changes: the service's speech is resampled to 24,000 samples a second, laid out on two tracks and
kept as FLAC.

Bricolage Grotesque, version 1.001: the face of the window's titles (D120), its Latin letters at weights 600 to 700, shipped as ui/bricolage-latin-600-700.woff2. Under the SIL Open Font License 1.1 (ui/BRICOLAGE-OFL.txt):

Copyright 2022 The Bricolage Grotesque Project Authors (https://github.com/ateliertriay/bricolage)
